## concept_biological_ship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aconcept_biological_ship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

—生物舰船使用特有的、职责分明的战舰分级，并有三个生长阶段。$NEW_LINE$—建造主要花费$i_food$而非$i_alloys$$NEW_LINE$—维护主要花费$i_food$维护费而非$i_energy$$NEW_LINE$由§Y£society£生物学§!科技主导而非§Y£engineering£宇航技术§!科技。$NEW_LINE$$h_line$§L大量活生生的肌肉纤维构成了活体的舰船，其中充满的生物发光细胞器可以释放高能化合物产生光热爆进行推进。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

- Biological ships use specialized combat ship classes with clearly defined roles and three growth stages. $NEW_LINE$- Construction costs primarily use $i_food$ instead of $i_alloys$ $NEW_LINE$- Upkeep costs primarily use $i_food$ upkeep instead of $i_energy$. $NEW_LINE$- Depend on §Y£society£Biology§! technologies instead of §Y£engineering£Voidcraft§! technologies. $NEW_LINE$$h_line$§LLiving vessels composed of massive, undulating muscle fibers filled with bioluminescent organelles release energy-rich compounds to produce bursts of light and heat for propulsion.§!

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:29`

## biogenesis_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abiogenesis_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H舰船组类型：['concept_biological_ship',生物]§!\n这些活体战舰天生就是顶级捕食者。它们半感性的躯体以永不满足的意志进行适应、狩猎和攻击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

§HShipset Type: ['concept_biological_ship', Biological]§!\nBorn as much as built, these living warships embody apex predation. Their semi-sentient biomass adapts, hunts, and strikes with insatiable will.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:35`

## biogenesis_02_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abiogenesis_02_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H舰船组类型：['concept_biological_ship',生物]§!\n为勇敢者而生，这些硬壳的星际舰船并不是在服役——它们生存着，与居住其中的无畏探险家们一同成长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

§HShipset Type: ['concept_biological_ship', Biological]§!\nBred for the bold, these hard-shelled starships do not serve - they endure, growing alongside the fearless explorers that call them home.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:36`

## humanoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahumanoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些为险恶的深空考验而生的舰船顽强又果决。它们怀着创造者的希望和梦想征服未知，直抵最遥远的繁星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:29`

### English

Sturdy and resolute, these vessels are built to endure the trials of deep space. Designed to conquer the unknown, they carry the hopes and dreams of their creators to the farthest stars.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:37`

## synthetics_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asynthetics_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些设计体现了不懈思维所蕴含的纯粹性。盘绕的散热结构能够分散来自超频主机的高能输出，确保完美运行，从而追求绝对的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:30`

### English

These designs reflect the purity of tireless minds. Heat-coiled hulls disperse energy from overclocked mainframes, ensuring flawless operation in the pursuit of absolute efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:38`

## mammalian_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amammalian_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些坚固的舰船经久耐用，体现了其制造者的坚韧精神。厚重的船体提供隔热与保护，冗余系统确保在最恶劣条件下也能生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:31`

### English

Built to last, these formidable vessels echo their makers' endurance. Dense hulls insulate and protect, while redundant systems ensure survival in the harshest conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:39`

## reptilian_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areptilian_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

方正实用，这种粗犷的设计让人联想到掠食者的坚韧。它们在虚空中的存在，证明了其创造者冷血般的顽强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:32`

### English

Boxy and utilitarian, this rugged design evokes the resilience of a predator. Their presence in the void is a testament to the cold-blooded tenacity of their makers.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:40`

## avian_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aavian_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

优雅而流畅，这些多功能的飞行器体现了飞行的美感。每一处曲线和轮廓都诉说着一个将太空视为天空延伸而非障碍的物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

Graceful and sleek, these versatile vessels embody the beauty of flight. Every curve and contour speaks of a species that views space not as an obstacle, but as an extension of the sky.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:41`

## arthropoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarthropoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几丁质或钢铁，形式或功能——并无区别。这些舰船建造的目的不在于激励，而在于持久，其层叠的船体是纯粹生存哲学的明证。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

Chitin or steel, form or function - there is no distinction. These ships are built less to inspire than to endure, their layered hulls a testament to a philosophy of pure survival.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:42`

## molluscoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amolluscoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

优雅而坚固，这些载体既是庇护所，也是利矛。在坚硬的外壳下，复杂的系统以静谧的效率调节着能量流动和养分循环。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:35`

### English

Graceful yet durable, these vessels are both shelter and spear. Beneath hardened shells, complex systems regulate power flow and nutrient cycles with quiet efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:43`

## fungoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afungoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些球茎状的船只唤起了真菌绽放的美丽。它们为自给自足而设计，内部的专门系统形成了复杂的网络，与菌丝的逻辑相呼应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

These bulbous vessels evoke the beauty of fungal blooms. Built for self-sufficiency, specialized systems form intricate internal networks, mirroring the logic of the mycelium.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:44`

## plantoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplantoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些翠绿的飞船不仅穿梭于星际之间，更在星辰中扎根。光合作用的舱体维持着平衡，调节着船内的生态系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:37`

### English

These verdant vessels do not merely travel the stars - they take root among them. Photosynthetic nacelles maintain equilibrium, balancing shipboard biomes.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:45`

## lithoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alithoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

历经时间与压力的锤炼，这些岩石般的容器体现了永恒。硅晶体矩阵支撑着核心系统，从塑造大自然的力量中汲取力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:38`

### English

Forged by time and pressure, these rocklike vessels embody permanence. Silicon-crystal matrices buttress core systems, drawing strength from the same forces that shape worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:46`

## necroid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anecroid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗而阴森，这些漂浮的大教堂是死亡与重生的纪念碑。它们那充满熵力的核心与仪式化的设计，低语着虚空之力与永恒的野心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

Dark and foreboding, these floating cathedrals are monuments to death and rebirth. Their entropic cores and ritualistic designs whisper of voidborne power and eternal ambition.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:47`

## nemesis_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anemesis_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

庞大而威严，这些设计令人臣服。强化的上层结构和冗余系统展现出无与伦比的统治力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

Massive and overbearing, these designs command submission. Reinforced superstructures and redundant systems project an aura of peerless dominion.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:48`

## aquatic_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaquatic_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些船只兼顾流线型和坚固耐受力，汇聚着海洋的力量。水性的内部构造与流线型的外形，体现了其创造者的优雅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

Flowing yet turgid, these ships channel oceanic power. Aqueous interiors and hydrodynamic contours embody the grace of their creators.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:49`

## toxoid_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoid_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些船只漏着废气，发出不祥的嘎嘎声，无视一切规章制度——但它们还能运行。以废物为燃料，靠着顽固的创造力连接在一起，它们在其他船只失效的地方能够坚持存活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

These vessels leak fumes, rattle ominously, and defy every regulation - but they run. Fueled by waste and held together by stubborn ingenuity, they endure where others fail.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:50`

## cybernetics_01_shipset_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acybernetics_01_shipset_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

镀铬闪耀，电路嗡鸣，反射神经已成过往。直接神经连接意味着零延迟，无限制——唯有纯粹、高辛烷值的精准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:43`

### English

Chrome gleams, circuits hum, and reflexes are a thing of the past. Direct neural uplinks mean no lag, no limits - just pure, high-octane precision.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:51`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_GRAPHICS_WEAVER_STAGE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

$weaver_class_plural_cap$是为舰队支援进行特化的改良生物舰船。它们极具适应性，既能助友舰一臂之力，也能干扰敌阵。

### English

$weaver_class_plural_cap$ are improved biological ships that specialize in fleet support. They are menacingly adaptive, and can run interference on the enemy while lending strength to allied vessels.

## TECH_UNLOCK_mauler_stage_1_CONSTRUCTION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATECH_UNLOCK_mauler_stage_1_CONSTRUCTION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$mauler_stage_1$§!\n$mauler_class_cap$是最基础的军用生物舰船，装有短射程武器和厚重大的甲壳装甲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

§H$mauler_stage_1$§!\nThe $mauler_class_cap$ is the most basic military ship, armed with close range weapons and thick carapace armor.

## tech_mauler_build_speed_desc

### 中文

这些孵化舱加速了营养丰富的生物舱内战舰胚胎的孕育过程。虽然简陋，但它们标志着一支舰队迈向并非建造、而是生长的第一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

These incubation chambers accelerate the gestation of embryonic warships within nutrient-dense biopods. Though rudimentary, they mark the first step toward a fleet that is not constructed, but grown.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:269`

## tech_harbinger_growth_2_desc

先进的孵化系统能够生产出具有卓越耐久性和火力的生物战舰。通过基因微调和优越的生长环境，这些战舰拥有无与伦比的韧性，足以在战场上占据主导地位。

## tech_stinger_build_speed_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_stinger_build_speed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些设施培育出流线型的敏捷生物飞船，专为精准和速度而设计。通过采用先进的生物舱和专用营养液，它们生产出能够进行精确高效打击的舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

These facilities grow sleek, agile biological ships designed for precision and speed. Employing advanced biopods and specialized nutrient solutions, they produce vessels capable of precise and efficient strikes.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:330`

## tech_stinger_growth_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_stinger_growth_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

精良的设施加速了增长周期，并提高了生物工程精度，从而打造出具有卓越机动性和精准度的舰船。这些提升使刺蜂在战术对抗中成为一股毁灭性的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Refined facilities accelerate growth cycles and improve bio-engineered precision, resulting in vessels with superior maneuverability and accuracy. These enhancements make the Stinger a devastating force in tactical engagements.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:334`

### 中文

生物飞船生长技术的巅峰之作，这些先进设施能够生产出速度和杀伤力无与伦比的刺蜂。优化的基因模板和尖端的孵化技术确保了这些飞船以迅捷而致命的精准度主宰任何战场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

植入腺体分泌一种强力的激素类固醇混合物，促使该舰船发育到下一个生长阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

Implanted glands secrete a potent mix of hormones and steroids, forcing this ship to advance to the next stage of growth.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:372`

## MAULER_PURSUIT_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAULER_PURSUIT_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

将舰船推进器官产生的化学废弃物进行循环利用可以提升耐久力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

Recycling the waste chemicals produced by the ship's motor organs allows for increased stamina.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:402`

## MAULER_PURSUIT_2

## MAULER_PURSUIT_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAULER_PURSUIT_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

舰船的在循环通路中添加易爆微粒可以进一步提升耐久力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

The addition of volatile motes in the recycling pathways of the ship provide further increases in stamina.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:404`

## MAULER_BITER_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAULER_BITER_1&lang=en)

腐蚀液体

### English

## MAULER_BITER_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAULER_BITER_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

植入的腺体调整了我们$mauler_class_plural_cap$体液的化学组成，使其具有腐蚀性并增强其潜在的破坏力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

Implanted glands modify the bodily fluids of our $mauler_class_plural_cap$, rendering them corrosive and enhancing their destructive potential.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:407`

## MAULER_BITER_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAULER_BITER_2&lang=en)

### 中文

强腐蚀液体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

Caustic Fluids

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:408`

## MAULER_BITER_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMAULER_BITER_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

将异星天然气添加进我们$mauler_class_plural_cap$的体液中可以进一步提升其造成伤害的能力，对敌方舰船的装甲更为有效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

The incorporation of exotic gases into the fluids of our $mauler_class_plural_cap$ further increases the damage they deal, in particular towards the armor of enemy ships.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:409`

## WEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_1&lang=en)

### 中文

发育信息素

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Developmental Pheromones

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:416`

## WEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

织蛛会释放出复杂的生化信号，加速附近有机体的成熟。这些发育信息素能提高生长速度和适应能力，确保舰队迅速进化以应对任何挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Weavers exude a complex array of biochemical signals that accelerate the maturation of nearby organic entities. These developmental pheromones enhance growth rate and adaptation, ensuring the fleet evolves rapidly to meet any challenge.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:417`

## WEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_2&lang=en)

### 中文

异星发育信息素

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Exotic Developmental Pheromones

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:418`

## WEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWEAVER_GROWTH_AURA_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

精密的生物工程技术增强了这些信息素的效力，显著加速了友军生物舰船的生长和优化。这种升级后的光环确保舰队以惊人的速度达到最佳性能，即使在激烈的战斗中也能如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

Refined bio-engineering amplifies the potency of these pheromones, dramatically accelerating the growth and optimization of allied biological ships. This upgraded aura ensures the fleet reaches peak performance with remarkable speed, even in the heat of battle.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:419`

## WEAVER_BOOSTER_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWEAVER_BOOSTER_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种有机装置增强了生物舰船之间的联系，提升了整个舰队的能量传输与协调能力。它的存在确保了战斗和资源管理效率的显著提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

This organic device strengthens the bond between biological ships, enhancing energy transfer and coordination across the fleet. Its presence ensures improved efficiency in combat and resource management.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:426`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_ARMOR_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种先进的生物装甲将坚韧的有机组织与纳米结构材料相结合，形成了一种能够抵御极端冲击的活体外壳。该外壳能够动态适应损伤，迅速再生，为生物飞船提供无与伦比的防护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

This advanced bio-armor integrates resilient organic tissues with nanostructured materials, creating a living carapace capable of withstanding extreme impacts. The carapace dynamically adapts to damage, regenerating swiftly and providing unparalleled protection for biological ships.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:453`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_ARMOR_3_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种生物装甲结合了坚韧的有机组织与塑钢加固材料，在强度和灵活性之间实现了卓越的平衡。这种混合结构能够提供对动能和能量攻击的出色防护，使其成为生物飞船的可靠选择。

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:467`

## BIO_ARMOR_5_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_ARMOR_5_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种甲壳是一种生物工程的奇迹，它将零素细丝融入有机组织中，形成了近乎坚不可摧的装甲。这种致密而适应性强的构成，即使面对最具破坏性的力量，也能提供无可匹敌的保护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

A marvel of bio-engineering, this carapace incorporates neutronium filaments within organic tissue, resulting in near-impervious armor. The dense yet adaptable composition offers unparalleled protection against even the most destructive forces.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:481`

## DRAGON_BIO_ARMOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADRAGON_BIO_ARMOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

生物装甲进化的巅峰之作，此甲壳的设计灵感源自远古时代的顶级掠食者。其层叠结构结合了可再生的有机物质和近乎坚不可摧的生物合金，创造出一种如启发它的生物一般凶猛且坚韧的防御。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

The pinnacle of bio-armor evolution, this carapace is inspired by ancient, apex predators. Its layered structure combines regenerative organic matter with nearly indestructible bio-alloys, creating a defense as fierce and unyielding as the creatures that inspired it.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:488`

### 中文

特化的肌肉组织产生能量场，形成保护性偏转护盾。尽管只能吸收很有限的伤害，但可以快速再生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

Specialized muscle tissue generates an energy field, forming a protective deflector shield. Although they can only absorb a limited amount of punishment, they are capable of rapid regeneration.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:535`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_SHIELD_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

精炼的肌肉组织能更有效地引导能量，从而强化偏导护盾。这些增强的系统能吸收更多伤害并更快地充能，在持久战中提供更强的防护能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

Refined muscle tissue channels energy more efficiently, strengthening deflector shields. These enhanced systems absorb more damage and recharge faster, offering greater protection in prolonged combat.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:542`

## BIO_SHIELD_3_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_SHIELD_3_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种先进的生物护盾技术整合了强化的、能量传导组织，具备更强的防护能力。它能有效抵御能量攻击和动能攻击，确保生物舰船在各种战斗环境中都能进行灵活防御。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

This advanced bio-shielding technology integrates reinforced, energy-conducting tissues with enhanced protective capabilities. It offers strong resistance against both energy and kinetic attacks, ensuring adaptable defense for biological ships in various combat situations.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:549`

### 中文

生物护盾技术的巅峰之作，这种强化肌肉组织能更高效地传导能量，增强护盾强度。它为能量和动能攻击提供了强有力的保护，确保在战斗中具备多方位防御能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

The pinnacle of bio-shield technology, this reinforced musculature conducts energy more effectively, enhancing shield strength. It offers robust protection against both energy and kinetic attacks, ensuring versatile defense in combat.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:556`

超适应性组织能够引导巨大的能量流，形成近乎坚不可摧的防御。这种先进的系统能够快速再生，即使在无情的攻击下也能保护生物飞船。

## DARK_MATTER_BIO_DEFLECTOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADARK_MATTER_BIO_DEFLECTOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种尖端生物护盾技术将暗物质融入有机组织，显著提升了能量传导和防御能力。这种特殊物质的注入使肌肉能够生成登峰造极的护盾，能够承受超乎寻常的打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

This cutting-edge bio-shielding integrates dark matter into organic tissues, dramatically enhancing energy conductivity and defensive capabilities. The infusion of this exotic substance enables the musculature to generate shields of unparalleled strength, capable of withstanding extraordinary levels of punishment.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:570`

## PSI_BIO_SHIELD_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APSI_BIO_SHIELD_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种先进肌肉组织融合了具有心灵感应活性的物质泽珞，能够以无与伦比的精确度和适应性生成护盾。其独特特性增强了生物护盾的韧性和再生能力，提供了近乎超自然的防御水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Infused with the psionically reactive substance Zro, this advanced musculature generates shields with unparalleled precision and adaptability. Its unique properties enhance both the resilience and regenerative capabilities of the bio-shield, providing an almost preternatural level of defense.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:577`

## PSI_BIO_BARRIER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3APSI_BIO_BARRIER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这种非凡的生物护盾通过特殊培育的组织驾驭灵能能量，创造出能够以不可思议的精确度适应来袭威胁的防御系统。其共振效应增强了护盾强度并加速了再生能力，使其成为抵御最不可预测攻击的强大保障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

This extraordinary bio-shielding harnesses psionic energy through specially cultivated tissues, creating defenses that adapt to incoming threats with uncanny precision. The resonance amplifies shield strength and accelerates regeneration, making it a potent safeguard against even the most unpredictable attacks.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:584`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:590`

## BIO_ARCHAEO_SHIELD_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_ARCHAEO_SHIELD_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

将护盾投射组织培植在远古文物结节上，该科技可以创造延留力场，在着弹瞬间将时间流速减缓。这一延缓增强了护盾消耗来袭攻击能量的能力，显著提升了防御韧性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

By cultivating shield-projection tissues around ancient artifact nodules, this technology generates suspension fields that slow time at the point of impact. This delay enhances the shield's ability to dissipate energy from incoming attacks, significantly improving defensive resilience.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:591`

## BIO_FISSION_REACTOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_FISSION_REACTOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过适当的生物工程改造，消化道可以充当生物核反应堆，实现基于诱发裂变的新陈代谢方式产生能量，但会产生有害的核废料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

With proper bio-engineering, a digestive tract can act as a biological nuclear reactor allowing for a metabolism based on induced fission, producing both power and harmful nuclear waste

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:599`

## BIO_FUSION_REACTOR_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_FUSION_REACTOR_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将具有核聚变能力的微生物群整合进舰船肠道的生态系统中，使其能在产生巨大能量的同时避免许多与裂变能源有关的危害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

By integrating microfauna capable of nuclear fusion into the biome of a bioship's gut, this system generates immense power while avoiding many of the hazards associated with fission-based energy sources.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:602`

### 中文

辐射定位

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

Radiolocation

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:748`

## BIOSHIP_SENSOR_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIOSHIP_SENSOR_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些感知器官利用类似雷达的生物机制在深空中发挥作用，使飞船具备一种基础的探测和追踪能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

These sensory organs make use of a biological equivalent of RADAR to function in deep space, allowing ships to have basic detection and tracking capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:749`

## BIOSHIP_SENSOR_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIOSHIP_SENSOR_2&lang=en)

### 中文

重力感受器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

Gravitic Sensors

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:750`

## BIOSHIP_SENSOR_2_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIOSHIP_SENSOR_2_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些感受器官用于探测空间中由飞船的存在而引起的重力变化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

These sensory organs have been developed to detect gravitational changes in space, such as those produced by the presence of a starship.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:751`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIOSHIP_SENSOR_3&lang=en)

### 中文

亚空间感受器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

Subspace Sensors

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:754`

## BIOSHIP_SENSOR_3_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIOSHIP_SENSOR_3_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一种可探测到飞船航行时产生的亚空间尾迹的感受器官。其灵敏度和作用距离远超此前发展的深空感受器官。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

Sensory organs that detect the subspace fluctuations left in the wake of traveling starships. Their sensitivity and range far exceeds any previous sensory organ developed for deep space.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:755`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_HYPER_DRIVE_1&lang=en)

### 中文

生物超空间场 I

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

Bio-Hyperlane Field I

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:767`

## BIO_HYPER_DRIVE_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_HYPER_DRIVE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过持续推动超空间来让舰船钻过星际超空间航道的狭小通道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:674`

### English

Constantly pushing against hyperspace allows for a ship to squeeze through the tight passages of interstellar hyperlanes.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:768`

## BIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1&lang=en)

### 中文

跃迁器官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

Jump Organ

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:773`

## BIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

跃迁奇观在现实织构中撕开舰船大小的裂口，实现近乎瞬时的跨星系移动。其间舰船所跨过的空间的本质我们仍然所知甚少。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

The Jump Organ cleaves ship-sized holes in the very fabric of reality, allowing for near-instantaneous movement between systems. The nature of the space the ship passes through on this journey is still poorly understood.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:774`

## BIO_PSI_JUMP_DRIVE_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_PSI_JUMP_DRIVE_1&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能跃迁器官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

Psionic Jump Organ

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:775`

## BIO_PSI_JUMP_DRIVE_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_PSI_JUMP_DRIVE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

灵能跃迁器官能够聚焦飞船与船员的灵能潜力，从而扭曲局部时空连续体，消除起始点与目标地点之间的距离——至少理论上是这样的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

The Psi Jump Organ acts as a focus point for the the psionic potential of the ship and crew to warp the local space-time continuum, eliminating the distance between the point of origin and the destination. Supposedly.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:776`

## tech_bio_hyper_drive_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_hyper_drive_1&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间移动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Hyperspace Travel

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:778`

## tech_bio_hyper_drive_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_hyper_drive_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就像蜘蛛网上的丝线一样，多维空间的其他维度在大多数恒星的重力井之间运动。在这些超空间航道间理论上是可以进行超光速旅行的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

Like the strands of a spider web, the extra-dimensional realm of hyperspace runs between the gravity wells of most stars. Faster than light travel is theoretically possible along these hyperlanes.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:779`

## tech_bio_hyper_drive_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_hyper_drive_2&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间航道突破点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Hyperlane Breach Points

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:780`

## tech_bio_hyper_drive_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_hyper_drive_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经证实，在太空中存在着特殊的“突破点”，通过这些点可以让航天器以最小的能耗进入超空间航道。这一发现将为新一代超级引擎的研发铺平道路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

We have identified the existence of points in space where a ship expends less energy to "breach" their way into a hyperlane. This will lead to a new generation of hyper drives.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:781`

## tech_bio_hyper_drive_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_hyper_drive_3&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间滑流

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Hyperspace Slipstreams

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:782`

## tech_bio_hyper_drive_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_hyper_drive_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

新一代超级引擎能够利用的亚空间滑流，这一突破性技术使其性能获得了显著提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

A new generation of hyper drives capable of accessing subspace slipstreams. This leads to dramatically improved drive performance.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:783`

## tech_bio_jump_drive_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_jump_drive_1&lang=en)

$BIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1$

## tech_bio_jump_drive_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_bio_jump_drive_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

革命性的$BIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1$可以在量子水平操控局部时空连续性，并重组至与目标地点完全相同的状态，于是这艘舰船基几乎在一瞬间完成了星系间的“跃迁”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

The revolutionary $BIO_JUMP_DRIVE_1$ manipulates the local space-time continuum at the quantum level, restructuring it to match the target destination. This process enables ships to appear to "jump" near-instantaneously between systems.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:785`

## BIO_SHIP_THRUSTER_4_DESC

### 中文

先进的聚变舱室为这艘飞船提供强大动力，使其在亚光速航行时具备无与伦比的机动性能和速度优势。

### 中文

暗物质太空运动器官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

Dark Matter Stellar Motor Organs

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:874`

## BIO_SHIP_THRUSTER_5_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_SHIP_THRUSTER_5_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在舰船的组织内嵌入暗物质使其可以在空间之间“游动”，通过违背常规物理定律和目前对推进的理解的方式进行移动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

Embedded clusters of dark matter within the ship's tissues enable it to "swim" through space, moving in ways that defy conventional physics and our current understanding of propulsion.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:875`

### English

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_DEFAULT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_DEFAULT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

所有生物舰船都具备基本的战斗本能，能够进行规避机动和目标计算。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

All biological ships have rudimentary combat instincts, capable of evasive maneuvers and targeting calculations.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:894`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_BASIC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_BASIC&lang=en)

### 中文

神经植入体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

Neural Implants

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:895`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_BASIC_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_BASIC_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些植入体直接与飞船的有机系统集成，能够实现快速决策和精确的战术协调。它们作为活体战斗计算机运行，增强了态势感知能力，并优化了战场表现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

These implants integrate directly with a ship's organic systems, enabling rapid decision-making and precise tactical coordination. Functioning as a living combat computer, they enhance situational awareness and optimize battlefield performance.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:896`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_ADVANCED

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_ADVANCED&lang=en)

### 中文

先进神经植入体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

Advanced Neural Implants

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:897`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_ADVANCED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_ADVANCED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

作为标准神经植入体的进化版本，这些系统提升了战术处理能力和决策速度。它们提供了更高的效率和协调性，使生物舰船能够迅速应对不断变化的战场条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

An evolution of standard neural implants, these systems enhance tactical processing and decision-making speed. They provide greater efficiency and coordination, allowing biological ships to respond swiftly to changing battlefield conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:898`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_SAPIENT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_SAPIENT&lang=en)

### 中文

智慧神经植入体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

Sapient Neural Implants

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:899`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_SAPIENT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_SAPIENT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些植入体承载着可以进行独立策略思考的智慧型生物智能。将有机直觉和人工理解结合，可以使生物舰船机动灵活地适应复杂的战斗场景，展现出接近全自动的决策能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

These implants house bio-sapient intelligence capable of independent strategic thought. By blending organic instinct with artificial reasoning, they enable biological ships to adapt dynamically to complex combat scenarios, exhibiting near-autonomous decision-making.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:900`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_AUTONOMOUS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_AUTONOMOUS&lang=en)

### 中文

自动神经植入体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Autonomous Neural Implants

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:901`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_AUTONOMOUS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_AUTONOMOUS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些系统完全自助运行。这些舰船内置的具有自我意识的生物智能使其可以在无需外部输入的情况下进行分析、策划并执行决策，实现前所未有的战斗效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

These systems operate with complete autonomy. Infused with self-aware bio-intelligence, they grant ships the ability to analyze, strategize, and execute decisions without external input, achieving unparalleled combat efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:902`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_PRECOG

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_PRECOG&lang=en)

### 中文

预知战斗直觉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

Precognitive Combat Instincts

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:903`

## BIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_PRECOG_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_COMBAT_COMPUTER_PRECOG_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些植入体利用灵能的力量为舰船提供潜意识直觉，使其可以在敌人行动发生之前就加以预知。这种近超自然能力让舰队拥有很大的决策空间，在最复杂的威胁面前也能做出近乎瞬间的反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Harnessing the power of psionics, these implants provide biological ships with precognitive insight, allowing them to anticipate enemy actions before they occur. This near-supernatural capability gives fleets a decisive edge, enabling split-second reactions to even the most complex threats.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:904`

## MINING_BIO_LASER_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMINING_BIO_LASER_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一种基于采矿无人机切割光束的生物学武器，能够轻松切穿厚实的甲壳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1047`

### English

A biological implementation on the cutting beams found on Mining Drones, this weapon can easily cut through thick carapace.

## BIO_POINT_DEFENCE_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_POINT_DEFENCE_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些器官结合特殊的外壳结构与肌肉的快速收缩，以先进的声致发光技术产生定向能量脉冲。这些脉冲射速快、能量高，非常适合用于对抗舰载机和导弹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1103`

### English

These organs use specialized shell structures in combination with fast-twitch muscles to produce directed energy pulses using an advanced form of sonoluminescence. The rapid fire and intensity of these pulses make them ideally suited for countermeasures against strike craft and missiles.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1253`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1109`

### English

## BIO_MASS_ACCELERATOR_1_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABIO_MASS_ACCELERATOR_1_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这些巨型舰炮主宰了飞船的生物学构造，对机动性和瞄准能力造成显著削弱。然而，此类武器纯粹的毁灭性威力足以弥补这些缺陷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1116`

### English

These enormous cannons dominate the ship's biology, causing significant impairments to movement and targeting. However, the sheer destructive power of such weapons more than compensates for these drawbacks.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1267`

### English

## tech_weaver_buffer_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

向友军舰船发射治愈性孢子，修复微小的船体损伤，在激烈的战场中增强韧性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1188`

### English

Restorative spores are fired at allied ships, mending minor hull damage and supporting resilience in the heat of battle.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1346`

## tech_weaver_buffer_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_3&lang=en)

### 中文

织蛛修复孢子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1189`

### English

Weaver Mending Spores

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1347`

## tech_weaver_buffer_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

强化再生外分泌可以释放强力治疗化合物在强大火力下支援友军。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1190`

### English

Enhanced restorative outputs deliver potent healing compounds to sustain allied ships under heavy fire.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1348`

## tech_weaver_buffer_4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_4&lang=en)

### 中文

织蛛适应孢子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1191`

### English

Weaver Adaptive Spores

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1349`

## tech_weaver_buffer_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

靶向释放高度适应性生物质可以修复显著损伤、促进器官再生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1192`

### English

Targeted bursts of highly adaptive biomatter repair significant damage while bolstering organic regeneration.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1350`

## tech_weaver_buffer_5

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_5&lang=en)

### 中文

织蛛智能孢子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1193`

### English

Weaver Intelligent Spores

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1351`

## tech_weaver_buffer_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

智能治疗体释放后可以主动寻的，修复致命损伤，大幅提高战场上的生存率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1194`

### English

Intelligent healing agents are dispatched, capable of seeking out and repairing critical damage points, drastically improving the chances of surviving in battle.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1352`

## tech_weaver_buffer_6

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_6&lang=en)

### 中文

织蛛神秘再生装置

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1195`

### English

Weaver Enigmatic Restoration Device

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1353`

## tech_weaver_buffer_6_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_buffer_6_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该生物支援武器能进行近乎瞬间修复再生，在最激烈的交火中确保友军存活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1196`

### English

This biological support weaponry is capable of near-instantaneous repairs and regeneration, ensuring allies endure even the fiercest engagements.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1354`

## BIO_HEALING_GUN_2_DESC

## tech_weaver_debuffer_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_debuffer_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有机抑制触须会释放不稳定脉冲，干扰敌方护盾并削弱其防御能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1240`

### English

Organic suppressor tendrils emit destabilizing pulses, disrupting enemy shields and weakening their defenses.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1401`

## tech_weaver_debuffer_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_debuffer_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

精密的压制系统能够放大干扰场，有效削弱护盾强度，降低敌方舰船的防御能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1242`

### English

Refined suppressor systems amplify disruption fields, effectively draining shield integrity and reducing the resilience of opposing vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1403`

## tech_weaver_debuffer_4

### English

## tech_weaver_debuffer_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_weaver_debuffer_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

强大的触须投射出广域压制场，通过扰乱敌方舰船的护盾和通讯系统，削弱整个敌方编队的战斗力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1244`

### English

Powerful tendrils project wide-area suppression fields, crippling entire formations of enemy vessels by disrupting both shields and communications.

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1405`

## SHIP_STAT_DESC_growth_speed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_STAT_DESC_growth_speed&lang=en)

### 中文

§E生物舰船积累生物质的速度，使其能够成熟为完全可运转的形态。较高的数值可加速生长过程，确保更快的部署。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1445`

### English

§EThe rate at which biological vessels accrue biomass, enabling their maturation into fully operational forms. A higher value accelerates the growth process, ensuring faster deployment.§!

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1617`

## SHIP_STAT_DESC_growth_threshold

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASHIP_STAT_DESC_growth_threshold&lang=en)

### 中文

§E生物舰船从原生阶段过渡到功能成熟期所需积累的生物质总量。较低的阈值能够让准备就绪更快完成。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/biogenesis_bioships_l_simp_chinese.yml:1446`

### English

§EThe requisite biomass accumulation a biological vessel must achieve to transition from its nascent stage to functional maturity. Lower thresholds allow for quicker readiness.§!

Source: `localisation/english/biogenesis_bioships_l_english.yml:1618`
